因言获罪、人权抗争与跨境迫害个案增补 Case Supplement: Speech Crimes, Rights Advocacy, Protest, and Forced Returns言論弾圧・人権活動・強制帰国の個案補遺표현 탄압·인권 옹호·강제 송환 사례 보충Casos de expresión, derechos humanos y retornos forzadosFallergänzung: Meinungsfreiheit, Menschenrechte und RückführungenComplément de cas : expression, droits humains et retours forcésSakstillegg: ytringsfrihet, menneskerettigheter og tvangsreturerAanvulling: expressie, mensenrechten en gedwongen terugkeerSupplemento casi: espressione, diritti umani e rimpatri forzati

最后更新：2026-07-02｜本页整理公开资料中的代表性个案。官方罪名仅为原案记录，不代表本站认可；公开信息有限的案件以“据报道”等方式标注。

一、快速补漏名单

本页汇总以下受到国际关注的人物和事件：刘晓波、秦永敏、彭立发、许志永、丁家喜、李翘楚、常玮平、黄雪琴、王建兵、余文生、许艳、谢阳、王全璋、江天勇、李和平、吴淦、周世锋、胡石根、高智晟、张展、陈品霖、董郁玉、黄琦、阮晓寰、陈玫、蔡伟、高瑜、陈杰人、耿潇男、李文亮、方斌、陈秋实、曹芷馨等白纸运动参与者、曾雨璇、陈云飞、桂民海、王炳章、董广平、姜野飞、卢思位、李明哲、杨恒均、王靖渝、乔鑫鑫、伊力哈木·土赫提、热依拉·达吾提、扎西文色、王怡、张春雷、许那、泰国遣返维吾尔人、黎智英、香港 47 人案、邹幸彤和李卓人。

二、详细个案清单

1. 刘晓波

分类：因言获罪 / 民主倡议

关键日期：2008-12-08 被拘押；2009-12-25 被判刑；2010-10-08 获诺贝尔和平奖；2017-07-13 羁押中病逝

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：判刑 11 年；2017 年在服刑期间因肝癌去世

事件摘要：刘晓波是《零八宪章》主要起草人之一。官方将其参与起草民主改革文本、发表亲民主文章等作为定罪证据。其案件是“文字、联署、和平倡议”被国家安全罪名处理的标志性案例，也是国际上最受关注的中国良心犯案件之一。

主要来源：Tom Lantos Human Rights Commission, Liu Xiaobo profile

2. 秦永敏

分类：因言获罪 / 民主倡议

关键日期：2015-05-06 被捕；2018 年被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：颠覆国家政权 / 煽动颠覆国家政权相关指控

判决或目前状态：判刑 13 年，另剥夺政治权利 3 年；据 PEN 资料仍在狱中

事件摘要：秦永敏是中国民主党共同创办人之一，长期推动政治改革、人权和民主建设，曾多次入狱。2018 年的重判显示，当局对有组织民主倡议和独立政治结社的持续压制。

主要来源：PEN America, Qin Yongmin profile

3. 彭立发 / 彭载舟

分类：因言获罪 / 街头抗议

关键日期：2022-10-13 北京四通桥抗议；2022-10 后失联；2025-07 报道称被秘密判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：据报道包括寻衅滋事、纵火等；官方判决文件未公开

判决或目前状态：据 CHRD 转引 AsiaNews 报道称被秘密判刑 9 年；具体羁押地点不明

事件摘要：彭立发在中共二十大前夕于北京四通桥悬挂反对清零政策和反对个人独裁的横幅，随后被带走。其行动被广泛视为白纸运动前的重要象征事件。由于官方没有公开完整司法材料，本案应标注“据报道”“公开信息有限”。

主要来源：Chinese Human Rights Defenders / AsiaNews, 2025-07-09, Peng Lifa reportedly sentenced; Human Rights Watch, 2023-11-19, Allow commemorations of White Paper protests

4. 许志永、丁家喜

分类：因言获罪 / 新公民运动

关键日期：2019-12 厦门聚会；2023-04-10 被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：许志永 14 年；丁家喜 12 年

事件摘要：两人长期参与新公民运动、公民权利倡议和非暴力公共讨论。2019 年底多名学者、律师和公民在厦门聚会后遭到追捕，案件被以“颠覆国家政权”处理。HRW 指出其审判秘密进行，并存在程序问题和遭虐待指称。

主要来源：Human Rights Watch, 2023-04-10, China: Quash Convictions of Prominent Rights Lawyers

5. 李翘楚

分类：因言获罪 / 女权与劳工

关键日期：2019-12-31 首次被传唤；2020-02-16 被指定居所监视居住；2024-02-05 被判刑；2024-08-03 预计刑满

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：判刑 3 年 8 个月，另剥夺政治权利 2 年；2024 年刑满后仍可能受限制

事件摘要：李翘楚关注女性权利与劳工权利。她因公开批评警方对许志永等人的处理、揭露酷刑与不当待遇而遭到起诉。国际特赦将其定性为因行使表达自由而被监禁。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2024-02-05, Li Qiaochu unjustly convicted

6. 常玮平

分类：维权律师 / 反酷刑发声

关键日期：2020-01 厦门聚会后曾被拘；2020-10 再次被带走；2023-06-08 被判刑；2024-07-08 刑满

官方罪名 / 指控：颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：判刑 3 年 6 个月；刑满后据人权组织资料仍受旅行限制和监控

事件摘要：常玮平曾代理反歧视、性别平等、劳工等案件。他在公开视频中描述自己遭受酷刑后，再次被拘押并最终被重判。该案典型地体现了“揭露酷刑者反被定罪”的模式。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2023-06-08, Chang Weiping sentence

7. 黄雪琴、王建兵

分类：女权 / 劳工 / 公民社会

关键日期：2021-09-19 在广州被捕；2024-06-14 被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：黄雪琴 5 年；王建兵 3 年 6 个月

事件摘要：黄雪琴是调查记者与 #MeToo 倡议者，王建兵长期关注职业病工人、残障者和劳工权益。检方将其参与线上人权学习、每周朋友聚会、关注社会正义议题等作为案件背景。国际特赦认为两人因和平社会倡议被惩罚。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2024-06-14, Huang Xueqin and Wang Jianbing convicted

8. 余文生、许艳

分类：维权律师 / 外交接触被打压

关键日期：2023-04-13 前往欧盟驻华机构活动途中被捕；2024-10-29 被判刑；2026-01-06 余文生上诉被驳回；2026-04-13 余文生刑满释放

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：余文生 3 年；许艳 1 年 9 个月；许艳 2026 年初刑满，余文生 2026-04-13 刑满

事件摘要：余文生曾代理多起敏感案件并公开呼吁宪政改革，曾于 2018–2022 年服刑。2023 年夫妻二人在前往会见欧洲外交官的活动途中被捕，后被以煽动颠覆定罪。该案显示与外国外交人员、人权机制接触也可能被刑事化。

主要来源：Front Line Defenders, 2026-04-14, Yu Wensheng released after completing sentence

9. 谢阳

分类：维权律师 / 最新一年

关键日期：2015 年 709 案中被拘；2022-01 再次被捕；2025-10 秘密审判；2026-03-23 被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动颠覆国家政权；寻衅滋事等曾被提及

判决或目前状态：2026-03-23 被判刑 5 年，并罚没 10 万元人民币；预计刑期至 2027 年 1 月左右（含已羁押时间）

事件摘要：谢阳长期代理宗教迫害、土地权利等敏感案件。HRW 报道称长沙中院 2026 年以“煽动颠覆国家政权”判处其 5 年，案件涉及秘密审理、长期审前羁押和禁止律师参与等严重程序问题。

主要来源：Human Rights Watch, 2026-03-23, Xie Yang sentenced to five years

10. 709 大抓捕整体事件

分类：709 律师打压

关键日期：2015-07-09 起全国范围展开；2015–2016 多人正式逮捕；后续影响延续至今

官方罪名 / 指控：颠覆国家政权、煽动颠覆国家政权、寻衅滋事等

判决或目前状态：国际特赦统计显示至少 248 名律师、活动人士被讯问、拘押或起诉；其他组织统计规模超过 300 人

事件摘要：709 大抓捕是习近平时期对独立法律职业和公民社会的标志性打击。律师、律所人员、家属和声援者遭指定居所监视居住、秘密拘押、电视认罪、吊销执照、限制出境和长期监控。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2016-07-07, China’s crackdown on human rights lawyers

11. 王全璋

分类：709 律师打压

关键日期：2015-08-03 被捕；2019-01 被判刑；2020-04 刑满释放

官方罪名 / 指控：颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：判刑 4 年 6 个月；释放后仍受限制、监控与家属骚扰

事件摘要：王全璋是 709 案中被羁押时间最长、外界长期无法确认生死和状态的律师之一。国际特赦指出，其家属长期无法探视，妻子李文足也持续受到骚扰。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2020-04-05, Wang Quanzhang released under restrictions

12. 江天勇

分类：709 律师打压

关键日期：2016-11 失踪/被拘；2017-11-21 被判刑；2019 刑满后仍受控制

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：判刑 2 年；释放后曾被长期监控、限制行动

事件摘要：江天勇曾代理法轮功、藏人、艾滋病维权等敏感案件，也为 709 律师家属奔走。2017 年被判 2 年，国际特赦称其受到不公正审判并面临酷刑风险。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2017-11-21, Jiang Tianyong two-year sentence

13. 李和平

分类：709 律师打压

关键日期：2015-07 被拘；2017-04-28 被判；2017-05 后回家但受监督

官方罪名 / 指控：颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：判刑 3 年，缓刑 4 年；剥夺政治权利并受长期监控

事件摘要：李和平是知名维权律师，曾代理宗教自由、强拆和言论自由案件。709 案中他长期被秘密羁押，后获缓刑，但释放并不等于自由，仍受监控和职业打压。

主要来源：Front Line Defenders, Case History: Li Heping; Amnesty International, 2016-07-07, China’s crackdown on human rights lawyers

14. 吴淦（屠夫）

分类：709 律师打压 / 公民行动

关键日期：2015-05 被捕；2017-12-26 被判刑；2023-05 刑满释放（据 CHRD 等资料）

官方罪名 / 指控：颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：判刑 8 年，另剥夺政治权利 5 年

事件摘要：吴淦以街头行动、网络声援、围观抗议等方式支持冤案受害者和维权律师。其量刑在 709 系列中很重，国际特赦记录其称在羁押中遭受酷刑。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2018-01-05, Wu Gan urgent action

15. 周世锋、胡石根、翟岩民等

分类：709 律师打压

关键日期：2015-07 后被捕；2016-08 天津系列审判

官方罪名 / 指控：颠覆国家政权 / 煽动颠覆国家政权等

判决或目前状态：周世锋 7 年；胡石根 7 年 6 个月；翟岩民缓刑等

事件摘要：锋锐律师事务所主任周世锋、基督徒民主活动人士胡石根和维权人士翟岩民等人在 709 案中被集中审理。官方宣传将维权法律行动描绘成“颠覆集团”，人权组织则认为审判带有强烈政治性和示范性。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2016-07-07, China’s crackdown on human rights lawyers

16. 高智晟

分类：维权律师 / 长期失踪

关键日期：2006 年被判缓刑；2007–2011 多次失踪并称遭酷刑；2014 出狱后被软禁；2017-08 后长期失踪

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动颠覆国家政权等

判决或目前状态：长期下落不明；家属多年无法确认安全状态

事件摘要：高智晟曾代理法轮功、地下教会、土地维权等案件，被称为中国最著名的维权律师之一。其经历包括酷刑指称、秘密羁押、软禁和长期失踪，是“出狱不自由”和强迫失踪的代表性案例。

主要来源：Tom Lantos Human Rights Commission, Gao Zhisheng profile

17. 张展

分类：公民记者 / 疫情信息

关键日期：2020-02 前往武汉报道；2020-05 被捕；2020-12-28 被判刑；2024-05 刑满；2024-08 再次被拘；2025-09 据多方声明称再被定罪

官方罪名 / 指控：寻衅滋事

判决或目前状态：2020 年案判刑 4 年；2024 年释放后又被拘押并于 2025 年再遭定罪（具体量刑需继续核验）

事件摘要：张展是律师出身的公民记者，曾在武汉封城期间发布医院、街头和市民生活视频。2020 年被判 4 年后，她在狱中长期绝食并健康恶化。2024 年释放后继续发声，很快再次被拘押，成为疫情信息控制延续到后疫情时期的代表。

主要来源：Reuters, 2020-12-28, Zhang Zhan four-year sentence; Media Freedom Coalition, 2025-10-13, Statement on Zhang Zhan

18. 陈品霖（Plato）

分类：公民记者 / 白纸运动

关键日期：2023-11 因纪录片被拘；2025-01-06 上海法院判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：寻衅滋事

判决或目前状态：判刑 3 年 6 个月

事件摘要：陈品霖制作纪录片《乌鲁木齐中路》，记录 2022 年白纸运动及反审查抗议。RSF 指出其在闭门审判后被判 3 年半，显示当局不仅打压抗议参与者，也追究记录和传播抗议记忆的人。

主要来源：Reporters Without Borders, 2025-01-08, Chen Pinlin sentenced

19. 董郁玉

分类：记者 / 新闻自由

关键日期：2022 年与日本外交官午餐时被捕；2024-11 被判刑；2025-11 上诉被驳回（据 CPJ 报道）

官方罪名 / 指控：间谍罪

判决或目前状态：判刑 7 年；截至 2025 年仍在狱中

事件摘要：董郁玉是《光明日报》资深编辑、专栏作者，长期与外国外交官、学者和媒体人正常交流。其被以间谍罪判 7 年，引发国际新闻界关注，显示与外国机构的普通职业接触也可能被安全化。

主要来源：Committee to Protect Journalists, 2025, Dong Yuyu profile

20. 黄琦

分类：记者 / 人权网站

关键日期：2016-11 被拘；2019-01 秘密审判；2019-07-29 被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：故意泄露国家秘密、为境外非法提供国家秘密等

判决或目前状态：判刑 12 年；健康状况长期引发关注

事件摘要：黄琦创办“六四天网”，长期记录访民、强拆、冤案和人权个案。2019 年重判被视为对独立人权记录工作的严厉警告。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2019-08-02, Huang Qi sentenced to 12 years

21. 阮晓寰（编程随想 / Program Think）

分类：网络自由 / 反审查

关键日期：2021-05 失踪/被拘；2023-02-10 被判刑；2023-03 家属公开案件

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：判刑 7 年，另剥夺政治权利 2 年

事件摘要：“编程随想”博客长期提供翻墙、信息安全和政治评论内容。RSF 报道称法院以其大量文章作为定罪基础。该案是技术知识、反审查教程和政治评论被刑事化的典型案例。

主要来源：Reporters Without Borders, 2023-04-03, Program Think/Ruan Xiaohuan sentenced

22. 陈玫、蔡伟、唐姓志愿者（端点星 / Terminus2049）

分类：网络存档 / 疫情审查

关键日期：2020-04-19 被拘；2020-06 后正式逮捕；后据人权组织资料以刑期抵扣释放

官方罪名 / 指控：寻衅滋事

判决或目前状态：曾遭指定居所监视居住/羁押；公开信息显示后续刑罚较短但经历秘密羁押

事件摘要：端点星志愿者在 GitHub 上备份被删除的疫情报道、采访和个人叙述。HRW 指出，他们因保存被审查信息遭拘押。该案说明“保存公共记忆”也会被视为政治风险。

主要来源：Human Rights Watch, 2020-04-27, Covid-19 activists and citizen journalists

23. 高瑜

分类：新闻自由 / 国家秘密

关键日期：2014-04 被捕；2015-04-17 被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：为境外非法提供国家秘密 / 泄露国家秘密

判决或目前状态：判刑 7 年；后因健康原因获保外就医

事件摘要：高瑜是资深记者。案件被认为与中共内部文件“九号文件”外泄有关，该文件攻击新闻自由、普世价值、民主和人权等观念。国际特赦将其视为以国家秘密法打压表达自由的代表。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2015-04-17, Gao Yu seven-year sentence

24. 陈杰人

分类：新闻自由 / 反腐报道

关键日期：2018 年被拘；2020-04-30 被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：寻衅滋事、敲诈勒索等

判决或目前状态：判刑 15 年，并处罚金

事件摘要：陈杰人曾揭露地方腐败、发表批评文章。RSF 称其 15 年刑期是习近平时期记者遭受的最严厉刑期之一。由于官方罪名包含经济类指控，网站表述时应写明“人权组织认为带有报复性”，不要简化成单纯“因言获罪”。

主要来源：Reporters Without Borders, 2020-05-05, Chen Jieren 15-year sentence

25. 耿潇男

分类：出版 / 声援知识分子

关键日期：2020 年被捕；2021-02 被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：非法经营罪

判决或目前状态：判刑 3 年

事件摘要：耿潇男是出版人，曾声援清华大学教授许章润等公共知识分子。PEN America 等组织认为其案件与言论和出版活动有关。由于官方罪名是“非法经营”，应作为“以非政治罪名处理公共表达”的案例。

主要来源：PEN America, 2021-02-11, Geng Xiaonan sentenced

26. 李文亮

分类：疫情吹哨 / 信息控制

关键日期：2019-12-30 发出疫情警示；2020-01-03 被警方训诫；2020-02-07 去世

官方罪名 / 指控：未被判刑；被警方以“散布谣言/不实言论”训诫

判决或目前状态：2020 年因感染 COVID-19 去世；后官方撤销训诫并道歉

事件摘要：李文亮医生因在同学微信群提醒 SARS 类病例而被警方训诫。他的死亡引发大规模网络哀悼和对言论审查的愤怒。虽然不是获刑案例，但非常适合放在“说真话被惩罚”的开篇案例。

主要来源：Reuters, 2020-02-07, Li Wenliang death sparked anger

27. 方斌、陈秋实

分类：公民记者 / 疫情信息

关键日期：2020-02 前后在武汉报道后失联；方斌 2023-04 获释；陈秋实 2021 年重新露面

官方罪名 / 指控：方斌据报道秘密判刑 3 年；陈秋实公开司法状态不透明

判决或目前状态：方斌已获释；陈秋实曾长期处于“被隔离/被监控”状态

事件摘要：方斌拍摄武汉医院和殡仪馆等现场，陈秋实以律师和公民记者身份在武汉发布视频。两人都在疫情早期信息最紧张时期失联，反映当局对非官方报道的高压控制。

主要来源：PBS/AP, 2023-04-30, Fang Bin to be released after three years; Wall Street Journal, 2021-10-01, Chen Qiushi resurfaced after 600 days; Human Rights Watch, 2020-04-27, Covid-19 activists and citizen journalists

28. 曹芷馨、李元婧、翟登蕊、李思琪等白纸运动参与者

分类：白纸运动 / 集体抗议

关键日期：2022-11-27 北京亮马桥/各地悼念与抗议；2022-12 至 2023-01 多人被拘；2023-04 前后部分取保

官方罪名 / 指控：寻衅滋事等

判决或目前状态：多人被刑拘或正式逮捕，后部分取保；仍有个案信息不完整

事件摘要：白纸运动源于乌鲁木齐火灾后的悼念和反清零抗议，随后扩展为对审查、封控和政治权利的公开表达。北京等地多名年轻女性和参与者被拘押，家属和朋友遭到警告。该群体应以“群体案件”方式呈现，避免遗漏普通参与者。

主要来源：Human Rights Watch, 2023-11-19, Allow commemorations of White Paper protests

29. 曾雨璇

分类：白纸运动 / 香港与内地连接

关键日期：2023-09 在香港被判刑；2023-10 刑满后被遣返回大陆；之后下落不明

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动罪（香港）

判决或目前状态：香港判刑 6 个月；被遣返回大陆后据 HRW 称遭强迫失踪

事件摘要：曾雨璇是中国大陆学生，在香港参与白纸运动和六四纪念相关表达后被香港法院以煽动罪判刑。刑满后她被遣返回大陆，随后外界无法确认其下落。该案显示香港国安/煽动罪与内地强迫失踪风险之间的连接。

主要来源：Human Rights Watch, 2023-11-19, Allow commemorations of White Paper protests

30. 陈云飞

分类：六四纪念 / 公开抗议

关键日期：2015-03 参加六四死难者纪念后被拘；2017-03 被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：寻衅滋事

判决或目前状态：判刑 4 年

事件摘要：陈云飞因组织或参与纪念六四遇难者的活动被判刑，是“纪念历史也被刑事化”的代表案例。对网站而言，它可以连接 1989 六四、历史记忆审查和当代维稳。

主要来源：Human Rights Watch, 2017-06-01, Own up to Tiananmen Massacre

31. 桂民海

分类：海外发声 / 被跨境绑架或强迫回国

关键日期：2015-10-17 在泰国失踪；2016 后出现在中国羁押体系；2020-02 被判刑；2025 年已被关押约十年

官方罪名 / 指控：为境外非法提供情报

判决或目前状态：判刑 10 年；羁押地点和会见权长期不透明；瑞典领事探视受限

事件摘要：桂民海是铜锣湾书店股东、瑞典公民，在泰国被带走后出现在中国羁押系统，并经历电视认罪。其案是中国跨境镇压、出版自由和香港言论空间收缩的国际标志性案例。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2025-10-15, Gui Minhai decade of secrecy

32. 王炳章

分类：海外民主运动 / 诱捕与绑架

关键日期：2002-06-27 在越南芒街被绑架至中国；2003-02-10 被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：间谍、恐怖主义等

判决或目前状态：无期徒刑；长期单独关押和健康问题受关注

事件摘要：王炳章是海外中国民主运动的重要人物。Tom Lantos 资料显示，他在越南边境城市被人强行带入中国，后经闭门审判被判无期。该案是“海外民主人士被诱捕/绑架回国”的早期典型。

主要来源：Tom Lantos Human Rights Commission, Wang Bingzhang profile

33. 董广平、姜野飞

分类：海外避难 / 强迫遣返

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动颠覆国家政权、非法越境等

判决或目前状态：董广平 3 年 6 个月；姜野飞曾被判 6 年 6 个月；后续均长期受限制

事件摘要：两人在泰国拥有联合国难民署相关保护文件并等待加拿大安置，却被遣返中国。前线卫士记录称，他们被带回后出现在中国国家电视台“认罪”。该案是非遣返原则被破坏与电视认罪的典型。

主要来源：Front Line Defenders, Dong Guangping case history

34. 卢思位

分类：海外逃亡 / 强迫遣返

关键日期：2021 年律师证被吊销；2023-07 在老挝被捕；2023-10 被遣返回中国；2025-08-05 刑满释放

官方罪名 / 指控：非法越境

判决或目前状态：判罚金并判刑 11 个月；2025-08 释放后仍受监控

事件摘要：卢思位曾代理“香港十二人”等敏感案件。他在试图经老挝前往美国与家人团聚时被老挝方面拘押并遣返中国。该案显示人权律师在出境、避难和家庭团聚过程中可能遭遇跨境合作式打压。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2023-10-04, Lu Siwei at risk after return from Laos; Associated Press, 2023, Laos deported Lu Siwei to China; Front Line Defenders, 2025-08-05, Lu Siwei released but under surveillance

判决或目前状态：判刑 5 年；刑满后回到台湾

36. 杨恒均 / 杨军

官方罪名 / 指控：间谍罪

判决或目前状态：死刑缓期二年执行；通常二年后可减为无期或有期徒刑

事件摘要：杨恒均是澳大利亚籍华人作家和亲民主博客作者，2019 年从美国抵达广州后被拘。Reuters 报道称其经过多年羁押和闭门审判后被判死缓，案件细节未公开。

37. 王靖渝

分类：海外发声 / 被第三国扣押风险

官方罪名 / 指控：中国方面因其质疑中印边境冲突死亡人数等网络言论追缉；迪拜方面具体法律程序复杂

判决或目前状态：未被遣返中国；最终离开迪拜

事件摘要：王靖渝是美国永久居民，因网络评论中国军人伤亡数字等被中国方面追缉。AP 报道其在迪拜被拘押数周，后被释放并飞往土耳其。该案说明海外言论可能引发第三国转机、引渡或遣返风险。

38. 乔鑫鑫 / 杨泽伟

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：据 Dui Hua 资料，被衡阳法院判刑 5 年

事件摘要：乔鑫鑫曾在海外推动反防火墙和网络自由倡议。RFA 报道其在老挝失踪后出现在中国羁押系统，Dui Hua 后续资料称其被判 5 年。该案与卢思位一样显示老挝成为中国异议人士的高风险地点。

主要来源：Radio Free Asia, 2023-08-14, Qiao Xinxin arrested after Laos disappearance; Dui Hua Foundation, 2024, Qiao Xinxin/Yang Zewei sentenced

39. 伊力哈木·土赫提

分类：民族权利 / 新疆

关键日期：2014-01-15 被带走；2014-09-23 被判刑；2019 获萨哈罗夫奖

官方罪名 / 指控：分裂国家

判决或目前状态：无期徒刑

事件摘要：伊力哈木·土赫提是维吾尔学者，长期倡导汉维对话和民族平等。国际特赦称其被以虚假“分裂主义”指控判处无期，审判严重不公。其案是新疆知识分子被打压的国际标志案例。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2016, Five facts about Ilham Tohti

40. 热依拉·达吾提（Rahile Dawut）

官方罪名 / 指控：分裂主义 / 危害国家安全相关罪名

判决或目前状态：无期徒刑，剥夺政治权利终身

事件摘要：热依拉·达吾提是研究维吾尔民俗和宗教文化的国际知名学者。Dui Hua 称可靠政府来源确认其无期徒刑。该案显示新疆文化研究、民俗记录和学术工作也可能被国家安全化。

主要来源：Dui Hua Foundation, 2023-09-21, Rahile Dawut life sentence confirmed

41. 扎西文色（Tashi Wangchuk）

判决或目前状态：判刑 5 年；刑满后仍可能受监控限制

事件摘要：扎西文色为藏语教育权利奔走，试图通过法律渠道维护藏语教学。他接受《纽约时报》纪录短片采访后被定罪，国际特赦认为其只是和平维护文化权利。

43. 张春雷长老

44. 许那

45. 泰国遣返 40 名维吾尔男子

官方罪名 / 指控：非个别刑事案件；涉及移民羁押和非遣返原则

判决或目前状态：至少 40 人被遣返中国；其后安全和待遇不透明

事件摘要：AP 报道泰国在国际呼吁下仍将至少 40 名被羁押十余年的维吾尔男子遣返中国。联合国和多国官员担忧他们回国后面临迫害、任意拘押和缺乏正当程序。

46. 黎智英

分类：香港国安法 / 新闻自由

关键日期：2020-08-10 被捕；2021《苹果日报》被迫停刊；2025-12 被定罪；2026-02-09 被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：串谋勾结外国势力、串谋发布煽动刊物等

判决或目前状态：2026-02-09 被判刑 20 年；已羁押多年

事件摘要：黎智英是《苹果日报》创办人和香港亲民主媒体人。AP 报道其 2026 年被判 20 年，是香港国安法下极重的媒体/政治案件之一。该案象征香港新闻自由和反对派空间急剧收缩。

主要来源：Associated Press, 2026-02-09, Jimmy Lai sentenced

47. 香港 47 人案：戴耀廷、黄之锋、何桂蓝、梁国雄、毛孟静等

分类：香港国安法 / 民主派初选

关键日期：2020-07 民主派初选；2021-01 大拘捕；2024-11-19 45 人被判刑

官方罪名 / 指控：串谋颠覆国家政权

判决或目前状态：45 人被判最高 10 年；戴耀廷 10 年，黄之锋 4 年 8 个月等

事件摘要：香港 47 人案是国安法实施后最大规模政治审判之一。被告参与或组织非官方民主派初选，官方称其计划通过赢得议席否决预算、迫使政府回应诉求。人权组织和多国政府批评案件将正常政治参与刑事化。

主要来源：Associated Press, 2024-11-19, Hong Kong 45 activists sentenced

48. 邹幸彤、李卓人及支联会相关人士

分类：香港国安法 / 六四记忆

关键日期：2021-09 被控煽动颠覆；2026-05 审判进入结案陈词阶段

官方罪名 / 指控：煽动颠覆国家政权等

判决或目前状态：长期还押待审；另有六四集会相关刑期或案件

事件摘要：邹幸彤、李卓人等与香港支联会长期组织六四烛光悼念。国际特赦 2026 年指出，相关审判围绕和平纪念天安门事件而展开，体现香港对历史记忆和结社自由的压制。

主要来源：Amnesty International, 2026-05-18, Tiananmen commemoration trial resumes

三、仍可继续补充但需二次核验

牛腾宇案、罗昌平“英烈法”案、任志强案、孙大午案、李旺阳死亡事件、蒋彦永医生去世前长期软禁、唐荆陵非暴力公民不合作案、李昱函律师案、王宇 / 包龙军出狱后限制、香港汤英杰案、周庭流亡后无法回港等，也可加入后续资料库。此类案例中有些官方罪名不是直接表达类罪名，建议正式发布时明确区分“官方指控”“家属说法”“人权组织判断”和“媒体报道”。

Last updated: 2026-07-02 | This page summarizes representative cases from public sources. Official charges are recorded as stated in the case record and do not imply endorsement by this site; cases with limited public information are marked with cautious wording such as "reportedly."

It is recommended to add the following topics as priority: Topics such as crimes for speech, New Citizens’ Movement, 709 Lawyers, Citizen Reporters of the Epidemic, White Paper Movement, cross-border persecution of overseas voices, Xinjiang/Tibet/Religious Freedom, Hong Kong National Security Law, etc. Please verify the name, date, sentence, release status and source link item by item before official release.

1. Quick list of missed leaks

The current website can give priority to supplementing these figures and events with high international attention: Liu Xiaobo, Qin Yongmin, Peng Lifa, Xu Zhiyong, Ding Jiaxi, Li Qiaochu, Chang Weiping, Huang Xueqin, Wang Jianbing, Yu Wensheng, Xu Yan, Xie Yang, Wang Quanzhang, Jiang Tianyong, Li Heping, Wu Gan, Zhou Shifeng, Hu Shigen, Gao Zhisheng, Zhang Zhan, Chen Pinlin, Dong Yuyu, Huang Qi, Ruan Xiaohuan, Chen Mei, Cai Wei, Gao Yu, Chen Jieren, Geng Xiaonan, Li Wenliang, Fang Bin, Chen Qiushi, Cao Zhixin and other participants of the White Paper Movement, Zeng Yuxuan, Chen Yunfei, Gui Minhai, Wang Bingzhang, Dong Guangping, Jiang Yefei, Lu Siwei, Li Mingzhe, Yang Hengjun, Wang Jingyu, Qiao Xinxin, Ilham Tohti, Reila Dawuti, Tashi Wangse, Wang Yi, Zhang Chunlei, Xu Na, Thailand repatriates Uighurs, Jimmy Lai, Hong Kong 47 people, Zou Xingtong and Li Zhuoren.

2. Detailed case list

1. Liu Xiaobo

Category: Convicted for speech/Democracy Initiative

Key dates: Detained on 2008-12-08; Sentenced on 2009-12-25; Won the Nobel Peace Prize on 2010-10-08; Died of illness in custody on 2017-07-13

Official crime/charge: Inciting subversion of state power

Judgment or current status:11 years in prison; died of liver cancer in 2017 while serving sentence

Event summary:Liu Xiaobo is one of the main drafters of Charter 08. Officials used his participation in drafting democratic reform texts and publishing pro-democracy articles as evidence of conviction. His case is a landmark case in which "words, joint signatures, and peace initiatives" were treated as national security crimes. It is also one of the most watched cases of Chinese prisoners of conscience in the world.

Main sources:Tom Lantos Human Rights Commission, Liu Xiaobo profile

2. Qin Yongmin

Category: Convicted for speech/Democracy Initiative

Key Dates: Arrested 2015-05-06; Sentenced 2018

Official charges/charges: Charges related to subversion of state power/inciting subversion of state power

Judgment or current status: Sentenced to 13 years in prison and deprived of political rights for 3 years; still in prison according to PEN

Event summary: Qin Yongmin is one of the co-founders of the China Democratic Party. He has long promoted political reform, human rights and democratic construction and has been imprisoned many times. The 2018 resentencing illustrates the authorities’ continued repression of organized democracy initiatives and independent political association.

Main sources:PEN America, Qin Yongmin profile

3. Peng Lifa/Peng Zaizhou

Category: Convicted for speech / Street protests

Key dates: 2022-10-13 Beijing Sitong Bridge protest; lost contact after 2022-10; 2025-07 reported to have been secretly sentenced

Official charges/charges: Reportedly include provoking quarrels and arson; official verdict documents have not been made public

Judgment or current status: According to CHRD, quoted by AsiaNews, he was secretly sentenced to 9 years in prison; the specific place of detention is unknown.

Summary of the incident:Peng Lifa was taken away after hanging a banner opposing the zero-clearance policy and personal dictatorship at Sitong Bridge in Beijing on the eve of the 20th National Congress of the Communist Party of China. His actions are widely regarded as important symbolic events in the lead-up to the White Paper Movement. Since the official has not disclosed complete judicial materials, this case should be marked as "according to reports" and "limited public information."

Main sources:Chinese Human Rights Defenders / AsiaNews, 2025-07-09, Peng Lifa reportedly sentenced; Human Rights Watch, 2023-11-19, Allow commemorations of White Paper protests

4. Xu Zhiyong, Ding Jiaxi

Category: Convicted for speech / New Citizens’ Movement

Key dates: 2019-12 Xiamen gathering; 2023-04-10 sentenced

Official crime/charge: Subversion of state power

Judgment or current status:Xu Zhiyong 14 years; Ding Jiaxi 12 years

Event Summary: Both men have long been involved in the New Citizens Movement, civil rights advocacy, and nonviolent public discussion. At the end of 2019, many scholars, lawyers and citizens were hunted down after gatherings in Xiamen, and the cases were treated as "subversion of state power." HRW noted that its trial was held in secret and was fraught with procedural issues and allegations of abuse.

Main sources:Human Rights Watch, 2023-04-10, China: Quash Convictions of Prominent Rights Lawyers

5. Li Qiaochu

Category: Crime for speech / Women’s rights and labor

Key dates: 2019-12-31 First summons; 2020-02-16 Placed under residential surveillance at a designated location; 2024-02-05 Sentenced; 2024-08-03 Estimated completion of sentence

Official crime/charge: Inciting subversion of state power

Judgment or current status: Sentenced to 3 years and 8 months, deprived of political rights for 2 years; may still be subject to restrictions after completion of sentence in 2024

Event Summary:Li Qiaochu is concerned about women’s rights and labor rights. She was prosecuted for publicly criticizing the police's handling of Xu Zhiyong and others, and exposing torture and improper treatment. Amnesty International characterized him as being imprisoned for exercising his freedom of expression.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2024-02-05, Li Qiaochu unjustly convicted

6. Chang Weiping

Category:Human Rights Lawyer / Anti-torture Speech

Key dates: 2020-01 Detained after a party in Xiamen; taken away again in 2020-10; sentenced on 2023-06-08; completed on 2024-07-08

Official crime/charge: Subversion of state power

Judgment or current status: Sentenced to 3 years and 6 months; still subject to travel restrictions and surveillance after completion of sentence, according to human rights organizations

Event summary: Chang Weiping has represented anti-discrimination, gender equality, labor and other cases. After describing his torture in a public video, he was detained again and eventually sentenced. This case is a typical example of the pattern of "those who expose torture are condemned instead."

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2023-06-08, Chang Weiping sentence

7. Huang Xueqin, Wang Jianbing

Categories: Feminism / Labor / Civil Society

Key dates:2021-09-19 Arrested in Guangzhou; 2024-06-14 Sentenced

Official crime/charge: Inciting subversion of state power

Judgment or current status:Huang Xueqin 5 years; Wang Jianbing 3 years 6 months

Event summary:Huang Xueqin is an investigative journalist and #MeToo advocate. Wang Jianbing has long been concerned about the rights of workers with occupational diseases, people with disabilities and labor rights. The prosecutor cited his participation in online human rights learning, weekly gatherings with friends, and attention to social justice issues as the background of the case. Amnesty International believes the pair were punished for their peaceful society initiatives.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2024-06-14, Huang Xueqin and Wang Jianbing convicted

8. Yu Wensheng, Xu Yan

Category:Human Rights Lawyers/Suppression of Diplomatic Contact

Key dates: 2023-04-13 Arrested on the way to EU institutions in China; 2024-10-29 Sentenced; 2026-01-06 Yu Wensheng’s appeal was rejected; 2026-04-13 Yu Wensheng was released after serving his sentence

Official crime/charge: Inciting subversion of state power

Judgment or current status:Yu Wensheng 3 years; Xu Yan 1 year 9 months; Xu Yan completed his sentence in early 2026, Yu Wensheng completed his sentence on 2026-04-13

Event summary: Yu Wensheng has represented many sensitive cases and publicly called for constitutional reform. He served a prison sentence from 2018–2022. The couple were arrested in 2023 on their way to an event to meet European diplomats and later convicted of inciting subversion. The case shows that contact with foreign diplomats and human rights mechanisms can also be criminalized.

Main sources:Front Line Defenders, 2026-04-14, Yu Wensheng released after completing sentence

9. Xie Yang

Category:Human Rights Lawyer / Latest Year

Key dates: Detained in Case 709 in 2015; arrested again in 2022-01; secret trial in 2025-10; sentenced on 2026-03-23

Official charges/charges: Inciting subversion of state power; picking quarrels and provoking trouble, etc. have been mentioned

Judgment or current status:2026-03-23 ​​Sentenced to 5 years in prison and fined RMB 100,000; expected sentence is until January 2027 (including time in custody)

Event summary:Xie Yang has long represented sensitive cases such as religious persecution and land rights. HRW reported that the Changsha Intermediate People's Court sentenced him to five years in 2026 for "inciting subversion of state power." The case involved serious procedural issues such as secret trials, long-term pretrial detention, and a ban on lawyers' participation.

Main sources:Human Rights Watch, 2026-03-23, Xie Yang sentenced to five years

10. The overall incident of 709 arrests

Category:709 Lawyer Suppression

Key Dates: Nationwide spread since 2015-07-09; many people were formally arrested in 2015–2016; the subsequent impact continues to this day

Official charges/charges: Subversion of state power, inciting subversion of state power, picking quarrels and provoking trouble, etc.

Judgment or current status: Amnesty International statistics show that at least 248 lawyers, activists have been interrogated, detained or prosecuted; other organizations count more than 300

Event summary: The 709 arrests are the signature crackdown on the independent legal profession and civil society during the Xi Jinping era. Lawyers, law firm staff, family members and supporters are subject to residential surveillance in designated locations, secret detention, televised confessions, license revocation, travel restrictions and long-term surveillance.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2016-07-07, China’s crackdown on human rights lawyers

11. Wang Quanzhang

Category:709 Lawyer Suppression

Key dates:2015-08-03 Arrested; 2019-01 Sentenced; 2020-04 Released after serving sentence

Official crime/charge: Subversion of state power

Judgment or current status: Sentenced to 4 years and 6 months; subject to restrictions, surveillance and family harassment upon release

Event summary:Wang Quanzhang is one of the lawyers who has been detained for the longest time in the 709 case, and the outside world has been unable to confirm his life, death and condition for a long time. Amnesty International pointed out that his family members have been unable to visit him for a long time, and his wife Li Wenzu has also continued to be harassed.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2020-04-05, Wang Quanzhang released under restrictions

12. Jiang Tianyong

Category:709 Lawyer Suppression

Key dates:2016-11 Missing/detained; 2017-11-21 Sentenced; 2019 Still under control after serving sentence

Official crime/charge: Inciting subversion of state power

Judgment or current status: Sentenced to 2 years; subject to long-term monitoring and restricted movement after release

Event summary: Jiang Tianyong has represented sensitive cases such as Falun Gong, Tibetans, and AIDS rights protection, and has also worked for the families of 709 lawyers. Sentenced to 2 years in 2017, Amnesty International said he had been unfairly tried and was at risk of torture.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2017-11-21, Jiang Tianyong two-year sentence

13. Li Heping

Category:709 Lawyer Suppression

Key dates: 2015-07 Arrested; 2017-04-28 Sentenced; 2017-05 later returned home but under supervision

Official crime/charge: Subversion of state power

Judgment or current status: 3 years in prison, 4 years suspended; deprivation of political rights and long-term monitoring

Event summary:Li Heping is a well-known human rights lawyer who has represented religious freedom, forced demolition and freedom of speech cases. In the 709 case, he was secretly detained for a long time and was later given a suspended sentence. However, release does not mean freedom and he is still subject to surveillance and professional suppression.

Main sources:Front Line Defenders, Case History: Li Heping; Amnesty International, 2016-07-07, China’s crackdown on human rights lawyers

14. Wu Gan (Butcher)

Category:709 Lawyer Suppression/Citizen Action

Key dates: Arrested on 2015-05; Sentenced on 2017-12-26; Released on 2023-05 (according to CHRD and other information)

Official crime/charge: Subversion of state power

Judgment or current status: 8 years in prison and deprivation of political rights for 5 years

Event Summary:Wu Gan used street actions, online support, and onlookers’ protests to support victims of unjust cases and human rights lawyers. His sentence was severe in the 709 series, and Amnesty International documented that he was tortured in custody.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2018-01-05, Wu Gan urgent action

15. Zhou Shifeng, Hu Shigen, Zhai Yanmin, etc.

Category:709 Lawyer Suppression

Key dates: 2015-07 post-arrest; 2016-08 Tianjin series of trials

Official charges/charges:Subversion of state power/inciting subversion of state power, etc.

Judgment or current status:Zhou Shifeng 7 years; Hu Shigen 7 years 6 months; Zhai Yanmin suspended, etc.

Event Summary: Zhou Shifeng, director of Fengrui Law Firm, Hu Shigen, a Christian democracy activist, and Zhai Yanmin, a human rights activist, were tried together in the 709 case. Official propaganda portrayed the rights-defending legal action as a "subversive group," while human rights groups viewed the trial as highly political and demonstrative.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2016-07-07, China’s crackdown on human rights lawyers

16. Gao Zhisheng

Category:Human Rights Lawyer/Long-Term Missing

Key dates: Sentenced to probation in 2006; disappeared multiple times from 2007–2011 and claimed to have been tortured; placed under house arrest after being released from prison in 2014; disappeared for a long time after 2017-08

Official charges/charges: Inciting subversion of state power, etc.

Judgment or current status: Long-term whereabouts unknown; family members unable to confirm safety status for many years

Event Summary: Gao Zhisheng has represented Falun Gong, underground churches, land rights protection and other cases, and is known as one of the most famous rights protection lawyers in China. His experience includes allegations of torture, secret detention, house arrest and long-term disappearance. It is a representative case of "no freedom after release from prison" and enforced disappearance.

Main sources:Tom Lantos Human Rights Commission, Gao Zhisheng profile

17. Zhang Zhan

Category: Citizen Journalist / Epidemic Information

Key dates: 2020-02 Reported to Wuhan; 2020-05 Arrested; 2020-12-28 Sentenced; 2024-05 Completed; 2024-08 Detained again; 2025-09 According to multiple statements, he was convicted again

Official crime/charge: Picking quarrels and provoking trouble

Judgment or current status: Sentenced to 4 years in the 2020 case; released in 2024, detained again and convicted again in 2025 (the specific sentencing needs to be verified)

Event summary: Zhang Zhan is a lawyer-turned-citizen journalist who published videos of hospitals, streets and citizens’ lives during the lockdown in Wuhan. After being sentenced to four years in 2020, she went on a long hunger strike in prison and her health deteriorated. After his release in 2024, he continued to speak out and was soon detained again, becoming a representative of the extension of epidemic information control into the post-epidemic period.

Main sources:Reuters, 2020-12-28, Zhang Zhan four-year sentence; Media Freedom Coalition, 2025-10-13, Statement on Zhang Zhan

18. Chen Pinlin (Plato)

Category: Citizen Journalists / White Paper Movement

Key dates: 2023-11 Detained for documentary; 2025-01-06 Shanghai court sentenced

Official crime/charge: Picking quarrels and provoking trouble

Judgment or current status: Sentence 3 years 6 months

Event summary: Chen Pinlin produced the documentary "Urumqi Middle Road" to record the 2022 White Paper Movement and anti-censorship protests. The RSF noted that his three-and-a-half-year sentence, following a closed trial, showed that authorities were not only repressing protest participants but also pursuing those who recorded and disseminated their memories.

Main sources:Reporters Without Borders, 2025-01-08, Chen Pinlin sentenced

19. Dong Yuyu

Category: Journalists / Press Freedom

Key dates: 2022 Arrested during lunch with Japanese diplomats; 2024-11 Sentenced; 2025-11 Appeal denied (as reported by CPJ)

Official Crime/Charge: Espionage

Judgment or current status: 7 years in prison; still in prison as of 2025

Event summary:Dong Yuyu is a senior editor and columnist of Guangming Daily. He has long-term normal communication with foreign diplomats, scholars and media people. His seven-year sentence on espionage charges sparked international press attention and showed that ordinary professional contacts with foreign agencies can also be securitized.

Main sources:Committee to Protect Journalists, 2025, Dong Yuyu profile

20. Huang Qi

Category:Journalists/Human Rights Websites

Key dates: 2016-11 detained; 2019-01 secret trial; 2019-07-29 sentenced

Official charges/charges: Intentionally leaking state secrets, illegally providing state secrets to overseas parties, etc.

Judgment or current status: 12-year sentence; long-term health concern

Event summary:Huang Qi founded the "June 4 Day Network" to record petitions, forced demolitions, unjust cases and human rights cases for a long time. The 2019 resentencing was seen as a stark warning to independent human rights record-keeping efforts.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2019-08-02, Huang Qi sentenced to 12 years

21. Ruan Xiaohuan (Program Think)

Category:Internet Freedom/Anti-Censorship

Key Dates: 2021-05 Disappearance/Detention; 2023-02-10 Sentenced; 2023-03 Family Disclosure Case

Official crime/charge: Inciting subversion of state power

Judgment or current status: Sentenced to 7 years in prison and deprived of political rights for 2 years

Event summary: The "Programming Caprice" blog has long provided circumvention, information security and political commentary content. RSF reports that the court relied on numerous articles as the basis for the conviction. The case is a classic example of the criminalization of technical knowledge, anti-censorship tutorials and political commentary.

Main sources:Reporters Without Borders, 2023-04-03, Program Think/Ruan Xiaohuan sentenced

22. Chen Mei, Cai Wei, volunteers surnamed Tang (Terminus2049)

Category: Online Archive / Epidemic Review

Key dates: Detained on 2020-04-19; formally arrested on 2020-06; later released with penalty of penalty of sentence according to human rights organization information

Official crime/charge: Picking quarrels and provoking trouble

Judgment or current status: Was placed under residential surveillance/detention at a designated location; public information shows that the subsequent sentence was shorter but the person was secretly detained.

Event summary: Endpoint volunteers back up deleted epidemic reports, interviews, and personal narratives on GitHub. HRW noted that they were detained for preserving censored information. The case illustrates that “preserving public memory” can also be seen as a political risk.

Main sources:Human Rights Watch, 2020-04-27, Covid-19 activists and citizen journalists

23. Gao Yu

Category:Press Freedom/State Secrets

Key dates: Arrested on 2014-04; Sentenced on 2015-04-17

Official charges/charges:Illegal provision of state secrets to overseas parties/leaking of state secrets

Judgment or current status: Sentenced to 7 years; later released on medical parole due to health reasons

Event Summary: Gao Yu is a senior journalist. The case is believed to be related to the leakage of "Document 9", an internal document of the Chinese Communist Party, which attacks concepts such as press freedom, universal values, democracy and human rights. Amnesty International regards this as a representative of the use of state secrets laws to suppress freedom of expression.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2015-04-17, Gao Yu seven-year sentence

24. Chen Jieren

Category:Press Freedom/Anti-Corruption Reporting

Key dates:Detained in 2018; sentenced on 2020-04-30

Official charges/charges: Picking quarrels and provoking trouble, extortion, etc.

Judgment or Current Status: Sentenced to 15 years in prison and fined

Event Summary:Chen Jieren once exposed local corruption and published critical articles. The RSF said the 15-year sentence was one of the harshest for a journalist under Xi Jinping. Because the official case also included financial allegations, this entry records both the official charges and the view of press-freedom organizations that the case was retaliatory.

Main sources:Reporters Without Borders, 2020-05-05, Chen Jieren 15-year sentence

25. Geng Xiaonan

Category: Publishing / Supporting Intellectuals

Key Dates: Arrested 2020; Sentenced 2021-02

Official crime/charge:Illegal business operation

Judgment or current status: 3 years in prison

Event summary: Geng Xiaonan is a publisher who has supported public intellectuals such as Tsinghua University professor Xu Zhangrun. Organizations such as PEN America have argued that their cases are related to speech and publishing activities. Since the official charge is "illegal business operation," it should be regarded as a case of "dealing with public expression on a non-political charge."

Main sources:PEN America, 2021-02-11, Geng Xiaonan sentenced

26. Li Wenliang

Category: Epidemic Whistleblowing/Information Control

Key dates: Issued epidemic warning on 2019-12-30; Reprimanded by the police on 2020-01-03; Died on 2020-02-07

Official charges/charges: Not sentenced; reprimanded by police for "spreading rumours/false statements"

Judgment or current status: Died of COVID-19 in 2020; official revocation of admonishment and apology later

Incident summary: Dr. Li Wenliang was reprimanded by the police for alerting his classmates about SARS-like cases in a WeChat group. His death sparked widespread online mourning and anger over censorship. Although it is not a prison sentence case, it is very suitable to be placed in the opening case of "telling the truth and being punished".

Main sources:Reuters, 2020-02-07, Li Wenliang death sparked anger

27. Fang Bin, Chen Qiushi

Category: Citizen Journalist / Epidemic Information

Key dates: Lost contact after reporting in Wuhan around 2020-02; Fang Bin was released in 2023-04; Chen Qiushi reappeared in 2021

Official charges/charges: Fang Bin reportedly secretly sentenced to 3 years in prison; Chen Qiushi’s public judicial status is opaque

Judgment or current status: Fang Bin has been released; Chen Qiushi has been in "isolation/monitoring" for a long time

Incident summary: Fang Bin filmed scenes such as Wuhan hospitals and funeral homes, and Chen Qiushi released videos in Wuhan as a lawyer and citizen journalist. Both men lost contact during the most stressful period of early information during the epidemic, reflecting the authorities' heavy-handed control of unofficial reporting.

Main sources:PBS/AP, 2023-04-30, Fang Bin to be released after three years; Wall Street Journal, 2021-10-01, Chen Qiushi resurfaced after 600 days; Human Rights Watch, 2020-04-27, Covid-19 activists and citizen journalists

28. Participants in the White Paper Movement such as Cao Zhixin, Li Yuanjing, Zhai Dengrui, and Li Siqi

Category: White Paper Movement / Collective Protest

Key dates: 2022-11-27 Beijing Liangmaqiao/Mournings and protests in various places; 2022-12 to 2023-01 many people were detained; 2023-04 some bails were released before and after

Official charges/charges: Picking quarrels and provoking trouble, etc.

Judgment or current status: Many people were detained or formally arrested, and some were later released on bail; there are still cases where the information is incomplete

Event Summary:The White Paper Movement originated from mourning and anti-clearance protests after the Urumqi fire, and later expanded into a public expression of censorship, blockade, and political rights. Many young women and participants were detained in Beijing and other places, and their families and friends were warned. The group should be presented as a “group case” to avoid missing ordinary participants.

Main sources:Human Rights Watch, 2023-11-19, Allow commemorations of White Paper protests

29. Zeng Yuxuan

Category:White Paper Movement/Connecting Hong Kong and the Mainland

Key dates: 2023-09 Sentenced in Hong Kong; 2023-10 deported to mainland China after serving sentence; subsequent whereabouts unknown

Official Crime/Charge: Sedition (Hong Kong)

Judgment or current status: 6-month sentence in Hong Kong; deported to mainland China, enforced disappearance, HRW says

Event summary: Zeng Yuxuan is a student from mainland China. She was sentenced by a Hong Kong court for incitement after participating in the White Paper Movement and expressions related to the June Fourth commemoration in Hong Kong. After serving her sentence, she was deported back to the mainland, and the outside world was unable to confirm her whereabouts. The case shows the connection between Hong Kong’s national security/incitement crimes and the risk of enforced disappearances on the mainland.

Main sources:Human Rights Watch, 2023-11-19, Allow commemorations of White Paper protests

30. Chen Yunfei

Category:June 4th Memorial/Public Protest

Key Dates: 2015-03 Detained after attending the commemoration of the June 4th victims; 2017-03 Sentenced

Official crime/charge: Picking quarrels and provoking trouble

Judgment or current status: 4 years in prison

Event summary:Chen Yunfei was sentenced for organizing or participating in activities to commemorate the victims of June 4th. This is a representative case of "commemorating history is also criminalized." The case illustrates how public commemoration of the 1989 Tiananmen Massacre has been treated as a matter of political control.

Main sources:Human Rights Watch, 2017-06-01, Own up to Tiananmen Massacre

31. Gui Minhai

Category: Speaking overseas/Being kidnapped across the border or forced to return to the country

Key dates: Disappeared in Thailand on 2015-10-17; appeared in the Chinese detention system after 2016; sentenced in 2020-02; detained for about ten years in 2025

Official charges/charges:Illegal provision of intelligence abroad

Judgment or current status: 10-year sentence; long-term opacity of detention location and access rights; limited Swedish consular access

Summary of the incident: Gui Minhai is a shareholder of Causeway Bay Bookstore and a Swedish citizen. After being taken away from Thailand, he appeared in the Chinese detention system and made a televised confession. His case is an international landmark case of China’s cross-border repression, freedom of the press, and the shrinking space for speech in Hong Kong.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2025-10-15, Gui Minhai decade of secrecy

32. Wang Bingzhang

Category: Overseas Democratic Movement / Entrapment and Kidnapping

Key dates: 2002-06-27 Kidnapped in Mong Cai, Vietnam and taken to China; 2003-02-10 Sentenced

Official charges/charges: Espionage, terrorism, etc.

Sentence or current status: Life sentence; prolonged solitary confinement and health concerns

Event summary:Wang Bingzhang is an important figure in the overseas Chinese democracy movement. Tom Lantos' information shows that he was forcibly brought to China in a border city in Vietnam and was later sentenced to life in a closed-door trial. This case is an early example of "overseas democrats being trapped/kidnapped and returned to their countries."

Main sources:Tom Lantos Human Rights Commission, Wang Bingzhang profile

33. Dong Guangping, Jiang Yefei

Category: Overseas asylum / forced repatriation

Key dates: 2015-10-28 Detained in Thailand; 2015-11-13 deported to China; 2015-12 televised confession; 2018-07 Dong Guangping was sentenced; 2019 Dong Guangping completed his sentence; 2026 Dong Guangping fled to Canada (according to AP report)

Official charges/charges: Inciting subversion of state power, illegal border crossing, etc.

Judgment or current status:Dong Guangping was sentenced to 3 years and 6 months; Jiang Yefei was sentenced to 6 years and 6 months; both will be subject to long-term restrictions in the future

Summary of the incident: The two people had relevant protection documents from the United Nations Refugee Agency in Thailand and were waiting for resettlement in Canada, but they were deported to China. Front Line Defenders documented that they were brought back and appeared on Chinese state television to "confess" their crimes. This case is a typical example of the violation of the non-repatriation principle and televised confessions.

Main sources:Front Line Defenders, Dong Guangping case history

34. Lu Siwei

Category: Overseas Fleeing/Forced Repatriation

Key dates: Lawyer certificate revoked in 2021; arrested in Laos in 2023-07; deported to China in 2023-10; released after serving sentence in 2025-08-05

Official Crime/Charge:Illegal border crossing

Judgment or current status: Fine and 11 months in prison; 2025-08 to remain under supervision upon release

Event summary:Lu Siwei has represented sensitive cases such as the "Hong Kong Twelve". He was detained by Laos and deported to China while trying to travel to the United States via Laos to reunite with his family. This case shows that human rights lawyers may encounter cross-border cooperative suppression during the process of leaving the country, asylum and family reunification.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2023-10-04, Lu Siwei at risk after return from Laos; Associated Press, 2023, Laos deported Lu Siwei to China; Front Line Defenders, 2025-08-05, Lu Siwei released but under surveillance

35. Li Mingzhe

Category:Overseas persons/arrested after returning to China

Key dates:2017-03-19 disappeared after entering Zhuhai from Macau; 2017-11-28 sentenced; 2022-04 returned to Taiwan after serving his sentence

Official crime/charge: Subversion of state power

Judgment or current status: Sentenced to 5 years in prison; returned to Taiwan after completion of sentence

Event summary: Lee Ming-che is a Taiwanese NGO worker who has discussed democracy, transitional justice and civil society with friends from mainland China on the Internet. Amnesty International pointed out that prosecutors used his online discussions on democracy, the disintegration of the Soviet Union and June Fourth as the basis for conviction.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2017-11-28, Lee Ming-che sentenced

36. Yang Hengjun/Yang Jun

Category: Overseas Chinese / Returned to China and accused of spies

Key dates: 2019-01 Detained at Guangzhou Airport; 2021 closed-door trial; 2024-02-05 sentenced to suspended death

Official Crime/Charge: Espionage

Judgment or current status: The death penalty is suspended for two years; usually it can be commuted to life or fixed-term imprisonment after two years

Summary of the incident: Yang Hengjun is an Australian-Chinese writer and pro-democracy blogger who was detained in Guangzhou after arriving from the United States in 2019. Reuters reported that he was given a suspended death sentence after years of detention and a closed-door trial. Details of the case have not been made public.

Main sources:Reuters, 2024-02-05, Yang Hengjun suspended death sentence

37. Wang Jingyu

Category: Overseas speaking / risk of being detained by a third country

Key dates: 2021-04 detained while in transit in Dubai; 2021-05-27 released and flown to Türkiye

Official charges/charges: China is pursuing people for their online comments such as questioning the death toll in the Sino-Indian border conflict; the specific legal procedures in Dubai are complex

Judgment or current status: Not deported to China; eventually left Dubai

Incident summary:Wang Jingyu is a permanent resident of the United States. He is wanted by China for commenting on the number of Chinese military casualties online. AP reports he was detained in Dubai for several weeks before being released and flown to Türkiye. The case illustrates how overseas speech may trigger risks of transfer, extradition or deportation to a third country.

Main sources:Associated Press, 2021-05-27, Wang Jingyu freed in Dubai

38. Qiao Xinxin / Yang Zewei

Category: Overseas anti-censorship / Return to China after disappearance in Laos

Key Dates: 2023-06 Disappeared in Laos; 2023-08 Confirmed in custody in Hunan, China; 2024 Sentenced according to Dui Hua information

Official crime/charge: Inciting subversion of state power

Judgment or current status: According to Dui Hua, sentenced to 5 years in prison by Hengyang Court

Event summary:Qiao Xinxin has promoted anti-firewall and Internet freedom initiatives overseas. RFA reported that he appeared in the Chinese detention system after disappearing in Laos, and subsequent information on Dui Hua stated that he was sentenced to 5 years. The case joins Lu Siwei in showing that Laos has become a high-risk location for Chinese dissidents.

Main sources:Radio Free Asia, 2023-08-14, Qiao Xinxin arrested after Laos disappearance; Dui Hua Foundation, 2024, Qiao Xinxin/Yang Zewei sentenced

39. Ilham Tohti

Category:Ethnic Rights / Xinjiang

Key dates: Taken away on 2014-01-15; Sentenced on 2014-09-23; Won the Sakharov Prize in 2019

Official crime/charge:Secession

Judgment or current status:Life sentence

Event Summary:Ilham Tohti is a Uyghur scholar who has long advocated Han-Uyghur dialogue and ethnic equality. Amnesty International said he was sentenced to life on false "separatist" charges and that the trial was grossly unfair. His case is an international landmark case of the suppression of intellectuals in Xinjiang.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2016, Five facts about Ilham Tohti

40. Rahile Dawut

Category: National Rights/Academic Freedom

Key Dates: 2017-12 Disappearance; 2018-12 Secret Trial; 2023-09 Public Information Confirming Life Sentence

Official charges/charges:Separatism/endangering national security related charges

Judgment or current status: Life imprisonment, deprivation of political rights for life

Event Summary: Reyila Dawuti is an internationally renowned scholar who studies Uyghur folk customs and religious culture. Dui Hua said reliable government sources confirmed his life sentence. The case shows that Xinjiang cultural research, folklore records and academic work can also be securitized by the state.

Main sources:Dui Hua Foundation, 2023-09-21, Rahile Dawut life sentence confirmed

41. Tashi Wangchuk

Category: Ethnic Rights / Tibetan Language

Key dates: Interviewed by The New York Times in 2015; arrested in 2016; sentenced on 2018-05-22; released on 2021-01-28

Official crime/charge: Inciting secession

Sentence or current status: 5 years in prison; may still be subject to supervision after completion of sentence

Summary of the incident: Tashi Wangchuk campaigned for the rights of Tibetan language education and tried to protect Tibetan language teaching through legal channels. He was convicted after being interviewed for a New York Times documentary, which Amnesty International said was a peaceful defense of cultural rights.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2018-05-22, Tashi Wangchuk five-year sentence

42. Pastor Wang Yi

Category: Religious Freedom / House Church

Key dates: 2018-12 Early Rain Covenant Church was arrested on a large scale; 2019-12-30 sentenced

Official charges/charges: Inciting subversion of state power, illegal business operations

Judgment or current status: 9 years in prison, 3 years deprivation of political rights and confiscation of property

Event summary:Wang Yi is the pastor of Early Rain Covenant Church in Chengdu. He has long spoken openly about religious freedom, human rights and constitutionalism. Amnesty International said he was simply exercising his right to religious belief and peaceful expression.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2019-12-30, Pastor Wang Yi sentence

43. Elder Zhang Chunlei

Category: Religious Freedom / House Church

Key dates: 2021-03-16 detained; 2024-07-24 sentenced; 2024-10 According to the church organization, the second trial upheld

Official charges/charges: Inciting subversion of state power, fraud

Judgment or current status: Sentence of 5 years; sentence until around 2026-03 (deducted based on time spent in custody)

Summary of the incident: Zhang Chunlei is an elder of Guiyang Charity Reformed Church. He was detained after going to the police station to inquire about detained Christians and was later sentenced to five years in prison for inciting subversion and fraud. This case illustrates that house church leaders and those who defend freedom of belief can also be dealt with on political charges.

Main sources:Dui Hua Foundation, 2024, Zhang Chunlei sentence

44. Xu Na

Category: Religious Freedom / Falun Gong

Key Dates: 2020-07 Arrested; 2022-01-16 Sentenced

Official charges/charges: Official charges of using a cult to undermine law enforcement, etc.

Judgment or current status: Sentenced to 8 years in prison and fined

Summary of the incident:Xu Na is an artist and Falun Gong practitioner. CSW reported that she was sentenced to eight years in prison for providing photos of the Beijing epidemic to overseas media and adhering to her Falun Gong beliefs. This case is suitable for supplementing "epidemic information, religious persecution and Falun Gong cases".

45. Thailand deports 40 Uighur men

Category: Forced Repatriation / Uyghurs

Key dates: Detained in Thailand since 2014; deported to China on 2025-02-27

Official charges/charges: Not an individual criminal case; involves immigration detention and non-removal principles

Judgment or current status: At least 40 deported to China; safety and treatment thereafter opaque

Event Summary:AP reports that Thailand is deporting at least 40 Uyghur men who have been detained for more than a decade to China despite international calls. United Nations and multinational officials are concerned that they will face persecution, arbitrary detention and a lack of due process upon their return.

Main sources:Associated Press, 2025-02-27, Thailand deports Uyghur men to China

46. ​​Jimmy Lai

Category:Hong Kong National Security Law / Press Freedom

Key dates: Arrested on 2020-08-10; forced to suspend publication of Apple Daily in 2021; convicted on 2025-12; sentenced on 2026-02-09

Official charges/charges:Conspiracy to collude with foreign forces, conspiracy to publish seditious publications, etc.

Judgment or current status: Sentenced to 20 years on 2026-02-09; has been in custody for many years

Event Summary: Jimmy Lai is the founder of Apple Daily and a pro-democracy media personality in Hong Kong. AP reports that his 20-year sentence in 2026 is one of the most serious media/political cases under Hong Kong’s national security law. The case symbolizes the sharp shrinkage of press freedom and opposition space in Hong Kong.

Main sources:Associated Press, 2026-02-09, Jimmy Lai sentenced

47. Hong Kong 47 cases: Dai Yaoting, Huang Zhifeng, Ho Kwai Lam, Leung Kwok Hung, Mo Mengjing, etc.

Category:Hong Kong National Security Law / Democratic primary election

Key dates: 2020-07 Democratic primary; 2021-01 mass arrests; 2024-11-19 45 people sentenced

Official Crime/Charge:Conspiracy to subvert state power

Judgment or current status: 45 people were sentenced to up to 10 years; Tai Yiu-ting 10 years, Joshua Wong 4 years 8 months, etc.

Event summary: The case of the 47 people in Hong Kong is one of the largest political trials since the implementation of the national security law. The defendants participated in or organized unofficial pro-democracy primaries, and officials said they planned to win seats to veto the budget and force the government to respond to demands. Human rights groups and many governments have criticized the case as criminalizing normal political participation.

Main sources:Associated Press, 2024-11-19, Hong Kong 45 activists sentenced

48. Zou Xingtong, Li Zhuoren and relevant persons in the stake association

Category:Hong Kong National Security Law/June 4th Memory

Key Dates: 2021-09 Accused of inciting subversion; 2026-05 trial enters closing argument stage

Official charges/charges: Inciting subversion of state power, etc.

Judgment or current status: Long-term remand pending trial; there are other sentences or cases related to the June 4th rally

Event summary:Zou Xingtong, Li Zhuoren and others have been organizing candlelight memorials for June 4th in conjunction with the Hong Kong Stakeholders Association. Amnesty International 2026 pointed out that the relevant trials were organized around the peaceful commemoration of the Tiananmen Incident and reflected Hong Kong's suppression of historical memory and freedom of association.

Main sources:Amnesty International, 2026-05-18, Tiananmen commemoration trial resumes

2. 詳細事例一覧

1. 劉暁波

カテゴリ: 言論/民主主義イニシアチブの罪で有罪判決

主要な日付: 2008 年 12 月 8 日に拘留。 2009 年 12 月 25 日に判決。 2010 年 10 月 8 日にノーベル平和賞を受賞。 2017年7月13日に拘留中に病気により死亡

公的犯罪/容疑: 国家権力の転覆を扇動すること

判決または現在の状況:懲役 11 年。服役中の2017年に肝臓がんのため死亡

イベント概要:劉暁波氏は憲章 08 の主な起草者の 1 人です。当局は、劉暁波氏が民主改革文書の起草や民主化推進記事の出版に参加していることを有罪の証拠として利用しました。彼の事件は、「言葉、共同署名、平和への取り組み」が国家安全保障上の犯罪として扱われた画期的な事件である。この事件は、世界で最も注目されている中国人良心の囚人事件の一つでもある。

主な情報源:Tom Lantos Human Rights Commission, Liu Xiaobo profile

2. チン・ヨンミン

カテゴリ: 言論/民主主義イニシアチブの罪で有罪判決

重要な日付: 2015 年 5 月 6 日に逮捕。 2018 年に判決

公式の告訴/告発: 国家権力の転覆/国家権力の転覆の扇動に関連した罪状

判決または現在の状況: 懲役 13 年の判決と 3 年間の政治的権利の剥奪。 PENによるとまだ刑務所にいる

イベントの概要: 秦永民は中国民主党の共同創設者の 1 人です。彼は長年にわたり政治改革、人権、民主主義建設を推進しており、何度も投獄されている。 2018年の憤りは、組織化された民主主義の取り組みと独立した政治結社に対する当局の継続的な弾圧を示している。

主な情報源:PEN America, Qin Yongmin profile

3. 彭力発/彭在州

カテゴリ: 言論 / 街頭抗議活動で有罪判決

重要な日程: 2022 年 10 月 13 日北京四通橋抗議活動。 2022-10 年以降に連絡が取れなくなった。 2025年から2007年に秘密裏に判決が下されたと報告されている

正式な容疑/ 容疑: 伝えられるところによると、口論や放火を誘発する行為が含まれます。公式判決文書は公開されていない

判決または現在の状況: AsiaNews が引用した CHRD によると、彼は密かに懲役 9 年の判決を受けた。具体的な拘留場所は不明。

事件の概要:彭力発氏は、第20回中国共産党大会の前夜、北京の四通橋でゼロクリア政策と個人独裁に反対する横断幕を掲げた後、連行された。彼の行動は、白書運動につながる重要な象徴的な出来事として広くみなされています。同当局者は完全な裁判資料を開示していないため、この事件は「報道によると」「限定的な公開情報」とマークされるべきである。

主な情報源:Chinese Human Rights Defenders / AsiaNews, 2025-07-09, Peng Lifa reportedly sentenced; Human Rights Watch, 2023-11-19, Allow commemorations of White Paper protests

4.徐志勇、丁佳熙

カテゴリ: 言論/新市民運動で有罪判決

主な日程: 2019 ～ 12 年 アモイ集会。 2023-04-10 判決

公的犯罪/容疑: 国家権力の転覆

判決または現在の状況:Xu Zhiyong 14 年。丁嘉熙 12年

イベントの概要: 両氏は、新市民運動、公民権擁護活動、非暴力の公開討論に長年携わってきました。 2019年末、厦門での集会後に多くの学者、弁護士、国民が追い詰められ、事件は「国家政権転覆」として扱われた。 HRWは、その裁判は秘密裏に行われ、手続き上の問題や虐待の申し立てが多々あったと指摘した。

主な情報源:Human Rights Watch, 2023-04-10, China: Quash Convictions of Prominent Rights Lawyers

5. 李喬忠

カテゴリ: 言論犯罪 / 女性の権利と労働

主要な日付: 2019 年 12 月 31 日 最初の召喚。 2020-02-16 指定された場所で居住監視下に置かれます。 2024-02-05 判決。 2024-08-03 文章完成予定日

公的犯罪/容疑: 国家権力の転覆を扇動すること

判決または現在の状況: 3 年 8 か月の懲役、2 年間政治的権利の剥奪。 2024年の刑期終了後も引き続き制限の対象となる可能性がある

イベントの概要:Li Qiaochu さんは女性の権利と労働者の権利について懸念しています。彼女は徐志勇らに対する警察の対応を公に批判し、拷問や不適切な扱いを暴露したとして起訴された。アムネスティ・インターナショナルは、彼が表現の自由を行使したために投獄されたと特徴づけた。

主な情報源:Amnesty International, 2024-02-05, Li Qiaochu unjustly convicted

6. チャン・ウェイピン

カテゴリー:人権弁護士 / 拷問反対のスピーチ

重要な日付: 2020-01 厦門でのパーティー後に拘束。 2020-10年に再び奪われた。 2023年6月8日に判決。 2024-07-08に完了

公的犯罪/容疑: 国家権力の転覆

判決または現在の状況: 3 年 6 か月の判決。人権団体によると、刑期終了後も渡航制限と監視の対象となっている

イベント概要: チャン・ウェイピンは、反差別、男女平等、労働その他の訴訟を代理してきました。自身の拷問を公開ビデオで説明した後、彼は再び拘留され、最終的には判決を受けた。この事件は「拷問を暴露した者が逆に断罪される」というパターンの典型例である。

主な情報源:Amnesty International, 2023-06-08, Chang Weiping sentence

7. 黄雪琴、王建兵

カテゴリ: フェミニズム / 労働 / 市民社会

重要な日付:2021-09-19 広州で逮捕。 2024-06-14 判決

公的犯罪/容疑: 国家権力の転覆を扇動すること

判決または現在の状況:黄学勤 5 年。王建兵 3年6ヶ月

イベントの概要:Huang Xueqin は調査ジャーナリストであり、#MeToo の支持者です。汪建兵氏は長年、職業病を患う労働者、障害者、労働者の権利について懸念を抱いてきた。検察側は事件の背景として、オンライン人権学習への参加、友人との毎週の集まり、社会正義問題への関心を挙げた。アムネスティ・インターナショナルは、二人が平和社会への取り組みを行ったために罰せられたと考えている。

主な情報源:Amnesty International, 2024-06-14, Huang Xueqin and Wang Jianbing convicted

8. ユウ・ウェンシェン、シュー・ヤン

カテゴリ:人権弁護士/外交的接触の抑制

重要な日付: 2023 年 4 月 13 日 中国の EU 機関に向かう途中で逮捕。 2024-10-29 判決。 2026-01-06 余文生氏の上訴は棄却された。 2026-04-13 余文生氏が刑期を終えて釈放された

公的犯罪/容疑: 国家権力の転覆を扇動すること

判断または現在のステータス:Yu Wensheng 3 年。シュー・ヤン 1歳9ヶ月。 Xu Yanは2026年初めに刑期を終え、Yu Wenshengは2026年4月13日に刑期を終えた。

イベント概要: ユー・ウェンシェン氏は多くのデリケートな訴訟を代理し、公に憲法改正を訴えてきました。 He served a prison sentence from 2018–2022.夫妻は2023年、欧州外交官らと会うイベントに向かう途中で逮捕され、後に転覆扇動の罪で有罪判決を受けた。この訴訟は、外国の外交官や人権団体との接触も犯罪化される可能性があることを示している。

主な情報源:Front Line Defenders, 2026-04-14, Yu Wensheng released after completing sentence

9. 謝楊

カテゴリ:人権弁護士 / 最新年

重要な日付: 2015 年に事件 709 で拘留。 2022年1月に再び逮捕。 2025年から2010年にかけて秘密裁判が行われる。 2026 年 3 月 23 日に判決

公的告発/告発: 国家権力の転覆を扇動すること。喧嘩を売ったり、トラブルを引き起こしたりすることが挙げられています。

判決または現在の状況:2026-03-23 懲役 5 年、罰金 10 万人民元の判決。予想される刑期は2027年1月まで（拘留期間を含む）

イベントの概要:Xie Yang は、長年にわたり、宗教迫害や土地の権利などのデリケートな事件の代理人を務めてきました。 HRWは、長沙中級人民法院が2026年に「国家権力転覆扇動」の罪で同氏に懲役5年の判決を下したと報じた。この事件には、秘密裁判、長期の公判前拘留、弁護士の参加禁止などの重大な手続き上の問題が含まれていた。

主な情報源:Human Rights Watch, 2026-03-23, Xie Yang sentenced to five years

10. 709人が逮捕された事件全体

カテゴリ:709 弁護士の弾圧

重要な日付: 2015 年 7 月 9 日以降、全国的に広がりました。 2015年から2016年にかけて多くの人が正式に逮捕された。その後の影響は今日まで続いています

公務/ 告発: 国家権力の転覆、国家権力の転覆を扇動する、喧嘩を売ったりトラブルを引き起こしたりなど。

判決または現在の状況: アムネスティ インターナショナルの統計によると、少なくとも 248 人の弁護士、活動家が尋問、拘留、または起訴されています。他の組織は 300 を超えています

事件の概要: 709 人の逮捕は、習近平時代の独立した法律専門家と市民社会に対する特徴的な弾圧です。弁護士、法律事務所のスタッフ、家族、支援者は、指定された場所での居住監視、秘密拘留、テレビでの自白、免許取り消し、渡航制限、長期監視の対象となる。

主な情報源:Amnesty International, 2016-07-07, China’s crackdown on human rights lawyers

11. 王全章

カテゴリ:709 弁護士の弾圧

重要な日付:2015-08-03 逮捕。 2019-01 判決。 2020-04 刑期を終えて釈放

公的犯罪/容疑: 国家権力の転覆

判決または現在の状況: 4 年 6 か月の判決。釈放時には制限、監視、家族への嫌がらせの対象となる

イベント概要:王全章は、709 事件で最も長期間拘留されている弁護士の 1 人であり、外の世界では長い間彼の生死、状態を確認することができませんでした。アムネスティ・インターナショナルは、彼の家族は長い間面会できず、妻の李文祖さんも嫌がらせを受け続けていると指摘した。

主な情報源:Amnesty International, 2020-04-05, Wang Quanzhang released under restrictions

12. ジャン・ティエンヨン

カテゴリ:709 弁護士の弾圧

重要な日付:2016 年 11 月 行方不明/拘留。 2017 年 11 月 21 日判決。 2019年 服役後もまだコントロール下にある

公的犯罪/容疑: 国家権力の転覆を扇動すること

判決または現在の状況: 懲役 2 年。釈放後は長期監視と移動制限の対象となる

イベント概要: Jiang Tianyong は、法輪功、チベット人、エイズの権利保護などのデリケートな事件を代理し、709 人の弁護士の家族のために働いてきました。 2017年に懲役2年の判決を受けたアムネスティ・インターナショナルは、同氏は不当な裁判を受けており、拷問の危険にさらされていると述べた。

主な情報源:Amnesty International, 2017-11-21, Jiang Tianyong two-year sentence

13. 李和平

カテゴリ:709 弁護士の弾圧

重要な日付: 2015-07 逮捕。 2017-04-28 判決。 2017-05 その後帰国したが監督下にあった

公的犯罪/容疑: 国家権力の転覆

判決または現在の状況: 懲役 3 年、執行猶予 4 年。政治的権利の剥奪と長期監視

イベント概要:リー・ヘピンは、信教の自由、強制取り壊し、言論の自由の訴訟を代理してきた著名な人権弁護士です。 ７０９事件では長期間秘密裏に拘束され、後に執行猶予付きの判決を受けた。しかし、釈放は自由を意味するものではなく、依然として監視と職業上の弾圧にさらされている。

主な情報源:Front Line Defenders, Case History: Li Heping; Amnesty International, 2016-07-07, China’s crackdown on human rights lawyers

14. ウー・ガン (肉屋)

カテゴリ:709 弁護士弾圧/市民活動

主要な日付: 2015 年 5 月に逮捕。 2017 年 12 月 26 日に判決。 2023-05 リリース (CHRD およびその他の情報による)

公的犯罪/容疑: 国家権力の転覆

判決または現在の状況: 懲役 8 年および政治的権利の剥奪 5 年間

イベントの概要:ウーガンは街頭行動、オンライン支援、野次馬の抗議活動を利用して、不当な事件の被害者や人権弁護士を支援しました。 709シリーズにおける彼の判決は重く、アムネスティ・インターナショナルは彼が拘留中に拷問を受けたことを文書化した。

主な情報源:Amnesty International, 2018-01-05, Wu Gan urgent action

15. 周世峰、胡思源、ザイ・ヤンミンなど

カテゴリ:709 弁護士の弾圧

重要な日付: 逮捕後 2015 年から 2007 年。 2016-08 天津シリーズ裁判

公的告発/告発:国家権力の転覆/国家権力の転覆扇動など。

判決または現在の状況:周世峰 7 年。胡思源 7歳6ヶ月。ザイ・ヤンミンの出場停止など

事件の概要: 豊瑞法律事務所所長の周世峰氏、キリスト教民主主義活動家の胡詩根氏、人権活動家のザイ・ヤンミン氏は、709 事件で一緒に裁判にかけられた。公式プロパガンダは権利擁護の訴訟を「破壊的集団」として描写したが、人権団体はこの裁判を極めて政治的で実証的なものとみなした。

主な情報源:Amnesty International, 2016-07-07, China’s crackdown on human rights lawyers

16.ガオ・ジーシェン

カテゴリ:人権弁護士/長期行方不明

主要な日付: 2006 年に保護観察の判決。 2007年から2011年にかけて複数回失踪し、拷問を受けたと主張した。 2014年に刑務所から釈放された後、自宅軟禁下に置かれた。 2017年8月以降長い間姿を消していた

公的な告訴/告発: 国家権力の転覆を扇動することなど。

判断または現在の状況: 長期にわたり行方不明。家族が長年安否を確認できない

イベント概要: 高志生氏は、法輪功、地下教会、土地権利保護などの訴訟を代理しており、中国で最も有名な権利保護弁護士の一人として知られています。彼の経験には、拷問、秘密拘留、自宅軟禁、長期失踪の申し立てが含まれます。 「釈放後の自由なし」強制失踪の代表的な事件である。

主な情報源:Tom Lantos Human Rights Commission, Gao Zhisheng profile

17. チャン・ザン

公的犯罪/容疑: 喧嘩を売ったりトラブルを引き起こしたりすること

判決または現在の状況: 2020 年の訴訟では懲役 4 年の判決。 2024年に釈放され、再び拘留され、2025年に再び有罪判決を受けた（具体的な量刑は検証する必要がある）

イベントの概要: チャン ザンは弁護士から市民ジャーナリストに転身し、武漢の封鎖中に病院、街路、市民生活のビデオを公開しました。 2020年に懲役4年の判決を受けた後、刑務所内で長期間のハンガーストライキを行い、健康状態を悪化させた。 2024年に釈放された後も発言を続けたが、すぐに再び拘束され、流行後の情報管理を拡大する代表者となった。

主な情報源:Reuters, 2020-12-28, Zhang Zhan four-year sentence; Media Freedom Coalition, 2025-10-13, Statement on Zhang Zhan

18. チェン・ピンリン（プラトン）

イベント概要: チェン ピンリンは、2022 年の白書運動と反検閲抗議活動を記録するドキュメンタリー「ウルムチ中道」を制作しました。 RSFは、非公開裁判の末の同氏の3年半の判決は、当局が抗議活動参加者を弾圧しているだけでなく、その記憶を記録し広めた者たちも追及していることを示していると指摘した。

19. ドン・ユユ

20. 黄斉

カテゴリ:ジャーナリスト/人権 Web サイト

イベントの概要:黄琦は、請願、強制取り壊し、不当な訴訟、人権訴訟を長期間記録するために「6 月 4 日ネットワーク」を設立しました。 2019年の再判決は、独立した人権記録の取り組みに対する厳しい警告とみなされていた。

21.阮暁環（プログラムシンク）

22. チェン・メイ、カイ・ウェイ、タンという姓のボランティア（Terminus2049）

イベントの概要: エンドポイントのボランティアは、削除された流行レポート、インタビュー、個人的な物語を GitHub 上でバックアップしています。 HRWは、彼らが検閲された情報を保存したために拘留されたと指摘した。この事件は、「国民の記憶を守ること」が政治的リスクともみなされる可能性があることを示している。

24. チェン・ジーレン

カテゴリ:報道の自由/汚職防止報道

判決または現在の状況: 懲役 15 年および罰金刑

イベントの概要:陳潔仁はかつて地元の汚職を暴露し、批判的な記事を発表しました。 RSFは、15年の懲役は習近平政権下のジャーナリストにとって最も厳しいものの一つであると述べた。公式の罪状には金銭的な罪も含まれているため、ウェブサイトはそれを説明する際に「人権団体は報復的であると信じている」と明記すべきであり、単に「言葉の罪で有罪判決を受けた」と単純化すべきではない。

25. 耿暁南

判決または現在の状況: 懲役 3 年

イベント概要: 耿暁南氏は、清華大学教授徐章潤氏などの公的知識人を支援してきた出版社です。 PENアメリカなどの団体は、自分たちの事件は言論活動や出版活動に関連していると主張している。正式な容疑は「違法な事業運営」であるため、これは「非政治的な容疑で公共の表現に対処した」事件と見なされるべきである。

26. 李文亮

正式な告訴/起訴: 判決は下されていません。 「噂や虚偽の陳述を広めた」として警察から懲戒処分を受けた

判断または現在の状況: 2020 年に新型コロナウイルス感染症により死亡。正式な勧告の取り消しと後日の謝罪

事件の概要: 李文亮医師は、WeChat グループで SARS のような事件についてクラスメートに警告したとして警察から懲戒処分を受けました。彼の死はネット上で広範囲にわたる追悼と検閲に対する怒りを引き起こした。実刑事件ではないが、「真実を語れば罰せられる」という冒頭の事件に非常にふさわしい。

記載と確認に関する注意事項: 「文章ではなく忠告」であることを明記する必要があります。

主な情報源:Reuters, 2020-02-07, Li Wenliang death sparked anger

27. 方斌、陳秋思

正式な告訴/ 容疑: 報道によると、方斌は密かに懲役 3 年の判決を受けた。陳秋石の公的司法上の地位は不透明である

判決または現在の状況: ファン・ビンは釈放されました。陳秋思氏は長期間「隔離・監視」下にある

事件の概要: 方斌は武漢の病院や葬儀場などの現場を撮影し、陳秋思は弁護士兼市民ジャーナリストとして武漢でビデオを公開した。両氏とも、感染症流行初期の情報提供が最も緊張する時期に連絡が取れなくなり、これは非公式報道に対する当局の強権的な統制を反映している。

28. 曹志新、李源京、財登瑞、李思奇などの白書運動の参加者

主な情報源:Human Rights Watch, 2023-11-19, Allow commemorations of White Paper protests

29. 曾玉軒

イベントの概要: Zeng Yuxuan は中国本土からの学生です。彼女は白書運動への参加と香港の六四記念日に関連した表現で扇動の罪で香港の裁判所から有罪判決を受けた。刑期を終えた後、彼女は本土に強制送還され、外の世界は彼女の所在を確認できなかった。この事件は、香港の国家安全保障/扇動犯罪と本土での強制失踪のリスクとの関連性を示している。

30. チェン・ユンフェイ

31. グイ・ミンハイ

公式告発/告発:海外への情報の違法提供

判決または現在の状況: 懲役 10 年。拘留場所とアクセス権の長期にわたる不透明さ。スウェーデン領事館へのアクセスが制限されている

事件の概要: Gui Minhai は銅鑼湾書店の株主であり、スウェーデン国民です。タイから連行された後、彼は中国の拘禁施設に出頭し、テレビで自白した。彼の訴訟は、中国の国境を越えた弾圧、報道の自由、香港における言論の余地の縮小に関する国際的に画期的な事件である。

32. 王冰章

正式な容疑/ 容疑: スパイ活動、テロ行為など。

刑期または現在の状況: 終身刑。長期の独房監禁と健康上の懸念

イベント概要:Wang Bingzhang は、海外の中国人民主化運動の重要な人物です。トム・ラントスの情報によると、彼はベトナム国境の都市で中国に強制連行され、その後非公開裁判で終身刑を宣告された。この事件は、「海外の民主主義者が罠にはめられ/誘拐され、自国に送還される」という初期の例である。

33. 東光平、ジャン・イーフェイ

カテゴリ: 海外亡命 / 強制送還

公的告発/告発: 国家権力の転覆、違法な国境越えなどの扇動。

事件の概要: 2 人はタイの国連難民機関からの関連保護書類を所持し、カナダでの再定住を待っていましたが、中国に強制送還されました。最前線の防衛隊は、彼らが連れ戻され、中国国営テレビに出演して犯罪を「告白」したことを記録した。この事件は、送金禁止原則違反と自白のテレビ放映の典型的な例である。
